Stripe Treasury for Platforms—Connected Accounts (United Kingdom)

Last modified: July 21, 2026

These Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Accountholder Terms of Service (“Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) Accountholder Terms”) supplement the General Terms, and together with the Stripe Financial Services Terms and the Stripe Treasury Terms, govern the use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services as a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder if User is located in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory. If User is a Stripe Connect Platform using the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for User’s own business purposes, these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) Accountholder Terms apply to User as a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder, and references to “Stripe Connect Platform” in these terms are also references to User. Capitalised terms that are not defined in these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) Accountholder Terms have the same meanings given to them in the General Terms, the Stripe Financial Services Terms and the Stripe Treasury Terms.

1. Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

User must be resident in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory to use the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. User’s Stripe Connect Platform will provide User access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services (if applicable). Subject to the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements and these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) Accountholder Terms, User and User’s Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users will be able to (a) add funds to User’s Financial Account via supported funding methods; and (b) transfer funds from User’s Financial Account to any account able to receive fund transfers at any financial institution. 

2. Use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

User acknowledges that User’s use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services may be subject to additional terms specific to User’s Stripe Connect Platform, which User’s Stripe Connect Platform will provide at the time User requests access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

3. Set up and Operation of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account.

3.1 Representation and Warranty.

User represents as of the date User requests to open a Financial Account, and warrants at all times during the Term, that, if User is an individual opening the Financial Account for a business that is a sole proprietorship, User is the sole owner of that business.

3.2 Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accounts

The Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account will contain one or more Financial Accounts. Financial Accounts are denominated in GBP, or such other currencies as Stripe may make available from time to time in its sole discretion.

4. Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users.

4.1 Instructions from Stripe Connect Platform.

User authorises Stripe to accept instructions from User’s Stripe Connect Platform with respect to Financial Account Transactions, manage User’s Financial Account and take other actions on User’s Financial Account. User must provide User’s Stripe Connect Platform with all information regarding User’s Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users, any agent appointed under an applicable power of attorney, and the electronic credentials (such as logins) for User’s Financial Account, and User must immediately provide User’s Stripe Connect Platform with updated information if that information changes. User must notify User’s Stripe Connect Platform at least 5 business days before making any change to (a) User’s form of ownership (such as changing from a sole proprietor to a limited liability company); or (b) User’s Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users.

4.2 Agents.

If User chooses to appoint an agent under a power of attorney to manage the Financial Account, the power of attorney must be in a form acceptable to User’s Stripe Connect Platform and RSP. RSP and User’s Stripe Connect Platform may rely on a copy of the power of attorney and are not required to confirm its validity.

4.3 Instructions.

If RSP chooses to comply with an instruction that User’s Stripe Connect Platform transmits, RSP may impose any condition that it deems reasonable prior to carrying out the instruction. RSP may refuse to comply with an instruction from User’s Stripe Connect Platform if Stripe believes that:

(a) the instruction is fraudulent or User did not authorise it;

(b) there are multiple or conflicting instructions;

(c) User’s Treasury for Platforms Authorised User or agent is exceeding their authority in giving the instruction; or

(d) complying with the instruction may expose RSP or its Affiliates to liability.

5. Operation of Financial Accounts

5.1 Accessing User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account.

User may access its Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account and the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services through the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard, and by any other means User’s Stripe Connect Platform makes available to User. User may use the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard to view User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account Information and conduct Financial Account Transactions, including adding funds to User’s Financial Account, transferring funds, and making payments from User’s Financial Account to third parties. If User uses other Services made available to User in connection with User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account or the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard, as applicable, additional terms may apply to those Services. User must use the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard only in compliance with the documentation that User’s Stripe Connect Platform makes available to User.

5.2 Account Security.

User must keep User’s account credentials and the devices on which User accesses User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account secure and confidential at all times. User must notify both User’s Stripe Connect Platform through the required channels and Stripe immediately at Treasury for Platforms-support@stripe.com if there has been any actual or suspected unauthorised access to User’s credentials. Failure to notify of any actual or suspected unauthorised access may impact User’s liability for any Financial Account Transactions that may occur. 

5.3 Representations and Warranties.

User represents on the date User first uses the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, and warrants at all times during the Term, that: 

(a) User is engaged in a commercial business enterprise (which may be as a sole proprietor) or a non-profit organisation organised and operating in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory;

(b) User resides in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory;

(c) User complies with all Financial Account Regulatory Requirements in connection with User’s use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services; and 

(d) User does not use the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to enable any individual or entity (including User) to benefit from any Prohibited or Restricted Business.

5.4 Legal Process.

User authorises RSP to comply with each Legal Process relating to User or User’s Financial Account that RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform receives, without needing to determine whether that Legal Process was validly issued or is enforceable. If a Transaction Hold is in effect, RSP may continue to charge all applicable fees, even if User’s Financial Account cannot be closed. RSP may charge User a fee for, and recover Stripe’s costs incurred in, complying with each Legal Process. If the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements permit, RSP will deduct those fees and costs from User’s Available Treasury for Platforms Balance.

6. Transactions on User’s Financial Account.

6.1 Adding Funds onto User’s Financial Account

RSP is not obligated to notify User promptly after funds are added into User’s Financial Account. User can confirm via the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard whether RSP has received a Financial Account Transaction that adds funds to User’s Financial Account.

6.2 Transfers from User’s Financial Account.

By initiating a transfer from User’s Financial Account via the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard, User authorises (a) User’s Stripe Connect Platform to instruct RSP to make the Financial Account Transaction User specifies; and (b) RSP to act on those instructions. RSP may deduct the amount of each transfer that User’s Stripe Connect Platform initiates or approves from User’s Available Treasury for Platforms Balance, even if the transfer has not yet been completed. RSP may refuse any transfer (i) by any method that RSP does not specifically permit for User’s Financial Account; or (ii) that does not include all information RSP requires. For any Confirmation of Payee requests, the Stripe Connect Platform may reply on User’s behalf, and RSP is entitled to rely on User’s Stripe Connect Platform’s response to the same extent it would rely on User’s response. 

6.3 Cross-Border Transfers Involving Currency Exchange.

If a transfer from User’s Financial Account requires a currency conversion, then the exchange rate used for that conversion will be determined and communicated to User by User’s Stripe Connect Platform. Where currency conversion is part of a transaction, any quoted payout amounts and associated fees are estimates, and may fluctuate without notice based on changes in the exchange rate that applies when the currency conversion takes place. RSP is not responsible for any losses User may suffer as a result of a currency exchange.

6.4 User’s Right to Stop Payment.

If User wishes to stop any scheduled or preauthorised transfer from User’s Financial Account, then User must give User’s Stripe Connect Platform instructions to do so in time for the Stripe Connect Platform to instruct Stripe accordingly at least 3 business days before the day on which the Financial Account Transaction is scheduled to be made. If User scheduled or preauthorised the transfer via the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard, then User may change or cancel the transfer via the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard at least 3 business days before the day on which the Financial Account Transaction is scheduled to be made.

6.5 Stripe’s Right to Refuse.

To the extent the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements do not prohibit the actions listed in this Section 6.5 (Stripe’s Right to Refuse), RSP may refuse to complete any Financial Account Transaction, including:

(a) if User’s Financial Account has insufficient funds to cover a Financial Account Transaction;

(b) if RSP reasonably doubts that it can collect any amounts owed to Stripe; 

(c) if RSP is unable to verify ownership of or other information about User’s Financial Account, or the individual or entity to or from whom the funds will be transferred;

(d) to protect the security of User’s Financial Account and Stripe’s systems; 

(e) if Stripe is concerned about potential fraud or other misconduct; or

(f) if the Financial Account Transaction would violate these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) Accountholder Terms or the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements.

6.6 Financial Account Transaction Limitations.

Limits on the number and value of Financial Account Transactions that User may make from User’s Financial Account may be imposed by RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform. RSP may allow Financial Account Transactions that exceed applicable limits, or temporarily reduce User’s limits from time to time without notice (unless the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements require otherwise). RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform may change the limits at any time.

7. Transaction Management.

7.1 Reviewing User’s Financial Account Transactions.

(a) Obligation to Review. User must review User’s Financial Account history and each Financial Account Transaction as soon as possible. If User believes that there is an error or discrepancy in a Financial Account Transaction, User must notify User’s Stripe Connect Platform promptly. If User believes an unauthorised Financial Account Transaction has been made on User’s Financial Account, then User must immediately notify User’s Stripe Connect Platform. If RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform requests, User must submit a written statement with details about the unauthorised Financial Account Transaction. If User fails to notify User’s Stripe Connect Platform as required, or User’s Stripe Connect Platform does not notify RSP in a timely manner, then losses, damages, or costs User suffers as a result of the unauthorised Financial Account Transaction may not be recoverable, or recovery may be more time consuming or difficult.

(b) Assistance in Investigations. User must assist RSP and User’s Stripe Connect Platform in the investigation and related prosecution of claims for unauthorised Financial Account Transactions by completing the appropriate statements and reports that RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform reasonably requests.

7.2 Liability Related to Financial Account Transactions.

To the extent permitted by Law, User is responsible for all Financial Account Transactions on User’s Financial Account, whether or not they are authorised. User acknowledges that Financial Account Transactions may be sent to the wrong account if User provides RSP with inaccurate or incomplete information (including due to compromise or fraud). To the maximum extent permitted by Law, the Stripe Parties will not be liable to User, User’s Affiliates or any third party for any losses, damages or costs arising out of or relating to:

(a) a failed Financial Account Transaction on User’s Financial Account, including if RSP decides to refuse to pay or decline any Financial Account Transaction or if a Financial Account Transaction fails due to circumstances outside of Stripe’s control;

(b) RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform completing a Financial Account Transaction or otherwise acting in reliance upon any incorrect or incomplete information provided or made available to RSP or User’s Stripe Connect Platform;

(c) RSP’s refusal to comply with User’s Stripe Connect Platform’s instructions under Section 4.3 (Instructions); or

(d) any correspondent bank’s negligence or failure, or funds lost in transit.

7.3 Reporting Unauthorised and Incorrectly Executed Transactions.

If User believes there are fraudulent or unauthorised transactions on User’s Financial Account, User must immediately report any such Financial Account Transactions to RSP by filing a dispute through the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard. 

If User believes that User’s Financial Account was subject to an incorrectly executed or defective charge, User must first contact the payee to resolve the dispute regarding the relevant Financial Account Transaction. If User is unable to resolve the dispute with the payee, then User must file the dispute through User’s Treasury for Platforms Dashboard. 

User must notify any incorrectly executed, defective or unauthorised Financial Account Transactions to RSP without undue delay, and no later than 13 months from the date the Financial Account Transaction appears on User’s Financial Account.

7.4 Determining Incorrectly Executed, Defective or Unauthorised Transactions.

When User notifies RSP of an incorrectly executed, defective or unauthorised Financial Account Transaction through the Treasury for Platforms Dashboard, RSP will evaluate the submitted information in accordance with Financial Account Regulatory Requirements. If RSP determines, in its sole discretion, that the Financial Account Transaction was incorrectly executed, defective or unauthorised, RSP will credit the amount of the applicable Financial Account Transaction back to User’s Financial Account. 

7.5 Liability for Unauthorised Transactions.

RSP will reimburse User for any unauthorised Financial Account Transaction where RSP determines that neither User nor a Treasury for Platforms Authorised User authorised the underlying Financial Account Transaction, provided that 

(a) User will be liable for the first £35 of any unauthorised Financial Account Transaction where RSP reasonably believes User should have been aware of the unauthorised use; 

(b) RSP will not be liable for any unauthorised transaction where (i) User or a Treasury for Platforms Authorised User used a Financial Account for an Unauthorised Purpose or with intent or gross negligence, failed to use the Financial Account in accordance with the terms of this Agreement, including to take all reasonable steps to keep account credentials and the devices on which User accesses User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account secure and confidential at all times, or the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines; or (ii) User failed to immediately notify RSP of the unauthorised use. 

If RSP reimburses User for an unauthorised or incorrectly executed Financial Account Transaction and subsequently determines that the relevant Financial Account Transaction was duly authorised and correctly executed, RSP may recoup or set off an amount equal to that reimbursed amount from User’s Financial Account.

8. Tax Reporting.

User is responsible for (a) determining User’s tax liability related to User’s Financial Account; and (b) reporting, withholding and remitting in a timely manner all taxes due to the appropriate Governmental Authority. User must promptly notify User’s Stripe Connect Platform of all changes related to User’s tax affairs, and provide all records and information that it or RSP requests to comply with the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements.

9. Fees and Security.

9.1 Fee Disclosure.

User’s Stripe Connect Platform is responsible for disclosing to User all applicable fees (if any) that apply to User’s use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. User’s Stripe Connect Platform will disclose those fees at the time User requests access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services and will disclose any changes before they take effect.

9.2 User’s Responsibility for Disputes.

To the maximum extent permitted by Law, User is liable to Stripe for all losses, damages and costs that Stripe incurs as a result of any dispute involving User’s Financial Account, including as a result of relying on User’s Stripe Connect Platform’s representations or instructions. User authorises Stripe to (i) deduct these losses, damages and costs from funds in User’s Financial Account; or (ii) set them off in accordance with Section 9.3 (Setoff) with or without prior notice to User.

9.3 Setoff.

RSP may deduct, recoup or setoff all fees User owes Stripe (if any) from funds in User’s Financial Account. Alternatively, Stripe may invoice User for some or all of those fees on a monthly basis in arrears, and User must pay the fees within 30 days after the date of the invoice.

10. Restricting or Closing User’s Financial Account.

10.1 Closing User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account.

Unless otherwise required under Financial Account Regulatory Requirements, after User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account is closed, RSP will enable User’s Stripe Connect Platform to provide User access to User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account history for 60 days.

10.2 Adverse Claims.

If there are conflicting instructions with respect to User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account or if there is any dispute regarding User’s Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account, then without limiting Stripe’s rights under these Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms, Stripe may refer the conflict or dispute to a court for resolution. If any person notifies Stripe of a dispute, Stripe does not need to decide if the dispute has merit before Stripe takes further action. Stripe may exercise any of these rights without notice to User.

11. Limitation of Liability.

To the extent that the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements invalidate or render unenforceable any limitations or exclusions of liability of RSP and its Affiliates under this Agreement with respect to failing to stop payment on a Financial Account Transaction, or paying an unauthorised Financial Account Transaction, then the Stripe Parties’ aggregate liability with respect to those circumstances will be limited to the face value of the Financial Account Transaction.

12. Definitions.

“Available Treasury for Platforms Balance” means the amount of funds that is available to be transferred or paid out of a Financial Account. 

“Confirmation of Payee” means the service to validate a payee’s account name before initiating a payment. 

“Connected Account” means (a) a Platform User that has a Stripe Account onboarded to a Stripe Connect Platform via the Stripe Connect Services; or (b) a Payout Recipient.

“Platform User” means, where User is acting as a Stripe Connect Platform, a user of User’s platform.

“Stripe Connect Platform” means a platform provider that uses the Stripe Connect Services. 

“Stripe Connect Services" means (a) if User is a Stripe Connect Platform, the Services that allow User to (i) create, manage and enable Services for User’s Connected Accounts, as described in the Stripe Connect Infrastructure Terms; and (ii) enable User or User’s Connected Accounts to use the Stripe Payments Services, as described in the Stripe Connect Terms (if applicable); or (b) if User is a Connected Account, the Services described in the Connected Account Agreement.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account” means an account that Stripe creates for a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder as part of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to enable the management of Financial Account(s). 

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder” means a Connected Account, or Stripe Connect Platform using the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for User’s own business purpose, who has successfully completed the onboarding requirements described in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Platform Terms.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account Information” means Personal Data or business information that a Stripe Connect Platform provides on behalf of its Connected Accounts to enable Stripe and its Affiliates to (a) determine the Connected Accounts’ eligibility to access the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services; (b) make the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services available to Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders; and (c) fulfill their responsibilities to Treasury for Platforms Transfer Networks.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines” means all product design, marketing, compliance, reporting and other guidelines and requirements established by a Stripe Entity from time to time in connection with the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services” means the Services that enable a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder to create and maintain a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account and Financial Account where the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder can (a) store, spend, and manage funds; and (b) make electronic payments and funds transfers to and from that account.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory” means the United Kingdom, Gibraltar, or such other jurisdictions Stripe permits, as further described in the Documentation.

"Treasury for Platforms Authorised User" means an individual that a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder authorises to use the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. 

“Treasury for Platforms Dashboard” means the user interface provided to enable a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder to manage its Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account.

“Treasury for Platforms Transfer Networks” means the electronic funds transfer networks the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services uses, credit card networks, and debit card networks.

“Unauthorised Purpose” means (a) a fraudulent or illegal purpose; (b) a consumer, personal, or household purpose; and (c) any purpose unrelated to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

Stripe Treasury for Platforms—Platform (United Kingdom)

Last updated: July 21, 2026

These Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms ( “Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms”) supplement the General Terms, the Stripe Financial Services Terms and the Stripe Connect Infrastructure Terms, and govern User’s ability to offer the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to Connected Accounts in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory. These Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms do not apply to User's use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for User’s own business purposes. If User uses the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for those purposes, then the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms will apply to User as a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder, except that references to “Stripe Connect Platform” in those terms are also references to User. Capitalised terms that are not defined in these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms have the same meanings given to them in the General Terms, the Stripe Financial Services Terms and the Stripe Connect Infrastructure Terms.

1. Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

User must not offer the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to ineligible Connected Accounts.

2. Access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

User must not offer, market or otherwise make the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services available to Connected Accounts located outside of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory. User must ensure that no Connected Account uses the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services outside of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory.

3. Onboarding Connected Accounts.

3.1 General Responsibility.

User must ensure that each Connected Account that wishes to access the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services successfully completes the onboarding requirements described in Section 3.2 (Information and Consent). Stripe must approve each Connected Account as part of the onboarding process before the Connected Account uses the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

3.2 Information and Consent. 

User must:

(a) submit, or ensure that each Connected Account submits, all Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account Information that Stripe requests to verify and screen the Connected Account’s eligibility to access the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, and confirm that the Connected Account is not engaging in a Prohibited or Restricted Business;

(b) ensure that each Connected Account User intends to onboard as a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder agrees to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms and the Connected Account Agreement before accessing the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. At Stripe’s request, User must provide proof as Stripe requires that the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms are binding on the Connected Account. If Stripe believes that User's existing acceptance process does not create a binding agreement between Stripe and the Connected Account, then, upon Stripe’s request, User must modify that process as Stripe requires;

(c) notify each Connected Account if Stripe approves its access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, and promptly notify a Connected Account that becomes a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder if Stripe suspends or terminates its access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services; and

(d) notify Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders of all changes to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms and ensure that those Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders agree to them. If any Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder does not agree to the modified Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms, then User must immediately stop making the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services available to that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder.

4. Responsibility for Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders.

4.1 Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms.

User must ensure that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders, along with their Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users, comply with the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms. Upon Stripe’s request, User must assist with enforcing the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms against Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users. User must ensure that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders implement and comply with any changes to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, which Stripe will communicate to User.

4.2 Responsibility for Activity.

User is responsible and liable to Stripe for all Activity on Connected Accounts in connection with the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, whether initiated by User or not, including all Financial Account Transactions, claims for reimbursement relating to authorised push payment fraud, associated fines, and any use of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services in a manner prohibited under these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms, the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms, or the Connected Account Agreement. User and the relevant Connected Account are jointly and severally liable to Stripe for amounts the Connected Account owes to Stripe, and Stripe may collect these amounts from User in accordance with this Agreement. If Stripe determines that User’s responsibility for Activity on Connected Accounts creates an unacceptable risk to Stripe, Stripe may exercise its remedies listed in Section 11.2 (Stripe Remedies).

5. Account Management and Support for Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders.

5.1 Dashboard for Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders.

User must provide and maintain a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard for use by Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders. 

5.2 Integration with Stripe API.

Promptly after Stripe notifies User that Stripe-hosted onboarding is available, User must integrate with the Stripe API according to the Documentation in a way that allows (a) a Stripe Entity to host onboarding of Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders; and (b) User or Stripe, at User's option, to be responsible for providing Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder account management and access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

5.3 Obligation to Pass On Information.

(a) If User elects to be responsible for providing Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder account management and access to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, then User must pass on to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders, without changes or deletions, all information that Stripe makes available for User to pass on to them. User must ensure that all information, including account balances, transaction history and customer service issues, that User passes from the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders to Stripe and its Affiliates is accurate and complete.

(b) User must ensure that Connected Account Data is passed to Stripe in the manner prescribed in the Documentation.

5.4 Communicating Fees.

User must clearly communicate to each Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder all fees (if any) that User charges that are related to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, and specify whether or not those fees are passthrough fees from Stripe. Stripe may review the fees User charges User's Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders in connection with the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to determine whether the fees comply with Stripe’s requirements, applicable Treasury for Platforms Transfer Network requirements, and Law. If Stripe determines the fees do not comply, User must immediately modify the fees and reimburse the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder as Stripe directs.

5.5 Cross-Border Transfers Involving Currency Exchange.

If a requested Financial Account Transaction requires a currency conversion, then the exchange rate used for that conversion will be determined as stated in the Documentation. Where currency conversion is part of a Financial Account Transaction, any quoted payout amounts and associated fees are estimates, and may fluctuate based on changes in the exchange rate that applies when the currency conversion takes place. User must clearly communicate to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder the relevant information about the currency conversion as required in the Documentation and under the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements.

5.6 Confirmation of Payee.

For any Confirmation of Payee requests, Stripe may permit or require User to reply on behalf of User's Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder, and Stripe is entitled to rely on User's response to the same extent it would rely on the response of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder. 

5.7 Support.

User must provide all support to Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users according to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines.

6. Compliance.

6.1 Generally.

User must: 

(a) make the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services available to Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders according to the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements; 

(b) ensure that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users access the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services in compliance with the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements; 

(c) not, and must ensure that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users do not, take or fail to take any action that could cause Stripe or any of its Affiliates to violate any Financial Account Regulatory Requirement; 

(d) upon request, provide Stripe with a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account on User's platform, and enable access to features (as needed) that Stripe may use to test and monitor User's use of, and compliance with, these Terms; and

(e) ensure that Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users use the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for business purposes only.

6.2 Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines.

User must (a) ensure that each Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder complies with all Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines; and (b) establish, maintain, and act according to a compliance program that will enable and ensure each Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder complies with the Financial Account Regulatory Requirements and the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines.

6.3 Marketing.

User must not distribute or share any marketing materials relating to the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services without Stripe’s prior approval. User must submit all marketing materials to Stripe using a process Stripe communicates to User. Stripe may provide template marketing materials to User, which User may use without further Stripe approval as long as the template marketing materials are not modified. 

6.4 Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Consents.

User must obtain all necessary rights and consents from each actual and prospective Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder to (a) provide Personal Data to Stripe and its Affiliates; (b) allow Stripe and its Affiliates to collect, use, retain and disclose that Personal Data; and (c) allow Stripe and its Affiliates to exercise their rights and perform their obligations under these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms and according to Stripe’s Privacy Policy. User must, when onboarding Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders, (a) disclose to them that User may provide Personal Data to Stripe, and its Affiliates, and that Stripe and its Affiliates may collect, use, retain and disclose Personal Data in accordance with Stripe’s Privacy Policy.

7. Data Use

7.1 Data Collection. 

User must obtain and maintain all necessary rights and consents under Law to provide the User Information that User provides to Stripe about each of User's (a) Principal Owners and employees, independent contractors and agents; and (b) Connected Accounts, Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders and Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users.

7.2 Securing Account Data.

User must secure Personal Data in User's possession or control, and User must ensure that User's Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders secure Personal Data in their possession or control. When stored or processed digitally, User must use appropriate organisational and technical measures to secure Personal Data in User's possession or control, including through industry-standard encryption. User must monitor, and User must ensure that User's Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders monitor, for suspicious, fraudulent and unauthorised Financial Account Transactions on each Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder’s Financial Account.

8. Disputes

8.1 Disputes

User must provide a channel through which Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders can submit disputes to User. User must immediately submit any disputes User receives to Stripe through the Stripe API or the Stripe Dashboard.  User must not interfere with any Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder’s ability to dispute a Financial Account Transaction.

8.2 Reporting Unauthorised Transactions.

If a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder believes that there are fraudulent or unauthorised transactions on a Financial Account, or that they have been the victim of an authorised push payment scam, User must immediately report any relevant Financial Account Transactions to Stripe by filing a dispute through the Stripe Dashboard or User must facilitate the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder in filing a dispute for any such Financial Account Transactions with Stripe.

8.3 Reporting Erroneous Transactions.

If User believes that a Financial Account was subject to an incorrectly executed or otherwise erroneous charge, the User must first contact the relevant payee regarding the relevant Financial Account Transaction. If the User is unable to resolve the matter with the relevant payee, then User must file the dispute through User's Stripe Dashboard or User must facilitate the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder in filing a dispute for the Financial Account Transaction through another channel that User provides. Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders must notify any incorrectly executed, erroneous or unauthorised Financial Accounts Transactions to Stripe within 13 months from the date the Financial Account Transaction appears on their Financial Account.

8.4 Determining Unauthorised Financial Account Transactions.

When a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder submits or reports an erroneous or unauthorised Financial Account Transaction, Stripe will evaluate the submitted information in accordance with Law. If Stripe determines, in its sole discretion, that the Financial Account Transaction was erroneous, incorrectly executed or unauthorised, Stripe will credit the amount of the applicable Financial Account Transaction back to the applicable Financial Account. User must abide by Stripe’s determination. User may not pass any costs or dispute fees User incurs as a result of any fraudulent or unauthorised Financial Account Transactions on to a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder.

9. Indemnification.

User will defend and indemnify the Stripe Parties from and against any Claim, and indemnify the Stripe Parties for all Stripe Losses (including claims for reimbursement relating to authorised push payment fraud) arising out of or relating to (a) inaccurate or incomplete information provided to a Stripe Entity by or on behalf of User or any Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder; (b) a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder’s breach of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms; (c) a negative balance or financial loss or damage caused by User or a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder; or (d) Financial Account Transactions. User authorizes Stripe to debit each User Bank Account or Stripe Account, at Stripe’s discretion, to cure any negative balance of a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder.

10. Liability.

10.1 Stripe Liability.

Notwithstanding anything in the Agreement to the contrary, Stripe will not be liable to User, Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder, Stripe Treasury for Platforms Administrators, or Treasury for Platforms Authorised Users for any losses, damages, or costs arising out of or relating to any Treasury for Platforms Payout Delay.

10.2 User's Liability for Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders.

Notwithstanding any provision of this Agreement (including any precedence or interpretation clause), there is no limitation or exclusion in relation to User's liability for Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders, or for User's failure to acquire binding acceptance by each Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder Terms in accordance with this Agreement.

10.3 Termination.

These Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms automatically terminate if the Stripe Connect Terms terminate for any reason.

11. Stripe Remedies.

11.1 Triggering Event. 

Stripe may exercise any one or more of the remedies stated in Section 11.2 (Stripe Remedies) if Stripe determines that a User Entity:

(a) has a negative Financial Account balance;

(b) is or is likely to become the subject of an Insolvency Event;

(c) has experienced or is likely to experience a deterioration of its business or financial condition that Stripe considers to be material;

(d) has breached or is likely to breach, or has caused or is likely to cause Stripe to breach, this Agreement (or any other Stripe services agreement with a Stripe Entity);

(e) has violated or is likely to violate Financial Account Regulatory Requirements;

(f) has caused or is likely to cause Stripe to violate Financial Account Regulatory Requirements;

(g) has experienced or is likely to experience a Change of Control;

(h) has, either itself or by way of its employees, agents, directors or contractors, initiated or submitted Financial Account Transactions or undertaken any other action that is or is likely to be fraudulent, suspicious or involve criminal activity;

(i) has, without Stripe’s prior consent, changed its business model (including services supplied by a User Entity to its customers), exposing Stripe to increased risk; or

(j) has acted in a manner or engaged in business, trading practice or other activity that presents an unacceptable risk.

11.2 Stripe Remedies.

If an event described in Section 11.1 (Triggering Event) occurs, then Stripe may do one or more of the following:

(a) establish, fund, and use a Reserve;

(b) debit each User Bank Account, Financial Account, or Stripe Account to cure any negative balance of a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder;

(c) suspend or terminate User’s ability to submit Financial Account Transactions; and

(d) refuse to process Financial Account Transactions.

12. Definitions

“Available Treasury for Platforms Balance” means the amount of funds that is available to be transferred or paid out of a Financial Account. 

“Confirmation of Payee” means the service to validate a payee’s account name before initiating a payment. 

“Financial Account” means the Stripe Account that Stripe creates for a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder as part of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. 

“Financial Account Transaction” means a transaction in a Financial Account that adds to or subtracts from the Available Treasury for Platforms Balance.

“Principal Owner” means, with respect to a legal entity, an individual who directly or indirectly, through any contract, arrangement, understanding, relationship, or otherwise, owns at least 25% of the equity interests of the legal entity.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account” means an account that Stripe creates for a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder as part of the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services to enable the management of Financial Account(s).

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account Activity” for purposes of these Stripe Treasury for Platforms (United Kingdom) - Platform Terms, means any action taken on or related to a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders’ Financial Account that User, a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder, or Stripe Treasury for Platforms Authorised User initiates, submits or performs, either through the Stripe Technology or through the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services, including any Financial Account Transaction.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder” means a Connected Account, or Stripe Connect Platform using the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services for User's own business purpose, who has successfully completed the onboarding requirements described in the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account Information” means Personal Data or business information that a Stripe Connect Platform provides on behalf of its Connected Accounts to enable Stripe to: (a) determine the Connected Accounts’ eligibility to access the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services; and (b) make the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services available to Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholders.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Administrator” means the individual that a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder appoints to manage the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services on its behalf. 

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Dashboard” means a user interface Stripe or a Stripe Connect Platform provides that enables a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder to manage its Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account and Financial Accounts.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Product Guidelines” means all product design, marketing, compliance, reporting and other guidelines and requirements established by a Stripe Entity from time to time in connection with the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services” means the Preview Services that enable a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder to create and maintain a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Account where the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder can create Financial Accounts to (a) store, spend, and manage funds; and (b) make electronic payments and funds transfers to and from its Financial Accounts.

“Stripe Treasury for Platforms Territory” means (a) the UK and Gibraltar, for Financial Accounts provided by Stripe Payments UK Limited; and (b) the EEA, for Financial Accounts provided by Stripe Technology Europe Limited.

“Stripe Losses” means all amounts awarded to the third party making a Claim, and all penalties, fines, and third-party costs (including legal fees) paid by the Stripe Parties.

“Treasury for Platforms Authorised User” means an individual that a Stripe Treasury for Platforms Accountholder authorises to use the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services. 

“Treasury for Platforms Payout Delay” means a delay to a Financial Account Transaction caused by (a) the unavailability of a Financial Partner, Governmental Authority, telecommunications provider or internet service provider; (b) incorrect information, such as a bank account number, provided to Stripe; (c) User's equipment, software, or other technology; (d) a delay or failure of a Financial Partner to settle a Financial Account Transaction to Stripe, including as a result of a Financial Partner’s default, insolvency, or bankruptcy; or (e) a Force Majeure Event.

“Financial Account Regulatory Requirements” means Law and the rules of the electronic funds transfer networks the Stripe Treasury for Platforms Services uses.
